Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the ode object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 214. The moduli space has dimension at most 870 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 214 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the ode structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 870. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on ode structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 655. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 258 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on ode structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 655. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 258. QED.
